Down 23 from 27. The client resigned in writing on 10/22 ('I've decided to move to a different management team') and ALTR has not replied in 8 days, so there is no partial credit for the relationship. The score sits at 4 rather than 0 because the exit is clean, not hostile: she thanked the team, she had just settled invoice #5 by asking for the Zelle request herself, and there is no dispute, chargeback or public complaint in the chat. It is a point below Amber Haze (5, exit unanswered 4 days) and level with Alex Paige Moore (4, exit unanswered 8 days) because the silence here is total and the ledger is worse: four invoices (#2-#5) have no receipt in chat and no closing invoice exists. What moved it from 27: (1) the exit itself; (2) zero owner response, continuing a 36-day non-billing silence from Derrek that the last two reports flagged with a 10/24 deadline; (3) her 10/21 video-call availability and her 10/19 'Everything uploaded ok?' went unanswered or half-answered, so the final interactions repeated the pattern that drove the 9/24 blow-up; (4) the 10/18 re-send of the September sexting-set folders proves the long-promised content work never happened. Offsets that do not change the outcome but belong on the record: first earnings/performance report delivered 10/16; fourth consecutive leak update; Ollie engaged with the 10/18 handover within 6 hours and asked for tags; Stephski sent the Zelle request on request; the never-ask-her-to-upload-to-ALTR's-Drive rule held.
